══ ARCHIVO: app\__init__.py ══════════════════════════════════════════════════

"""Flask application factory."""

from flask import Flask
from flask_caching import Cache

cache = Cache()


def create_app():
    app = Flask(__name__, template_folder="templates", static_folder="static")
    app.config.update(
        CACHE_TYPE="SimpleCache",
        CACHE_DEFAULT_TIMEOUT=3600,
    )
    cache.init_app(app)

    from app.routes.main import bp as main_bp
    from app.routes.exports import bp as exports_bp
    from app.routes.banco import bp as banco_bp
    app.register_blueprint(main_bp)
    app.register_blueprint(exports_bp)
    app.register_blueprint(banco_bp)

    return app

══ ARCHIVO: app\routes\banco.py ══════════════════════════════════════════════

"""Banco de Tesis: los 54 experimentos publicados en el Capítulo 4 y el Apéndice C.

Página de solo lectura. Los resultados provienen de ``src/benchmark_data/resultados_tesis.json``
(generado por ``scripts/build_published.py`` a partir de ``benchmark/``); aquí no se entrena nada.
"""

import json
import os

from flask import Blueprint, render_template

bp = Blueprint("banco", __name__)

_PUB_PATH = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))),
                         "src", "benchmark_data", "resultados_tesis.json")
_PUB = None
_CTX = None

MODELS6 = ["SARIMA", "Holt-Winters", "Regresión Lineal", "Random Forest", "RNN", "LSTM"]
ABBR = {"Naive": "Naive", "SARIMA": "SARIMA", "Holt-Winters": "H-W", "Regresión Lineal": "Reg. Lin.",
        "Random Forest": "R. Forest", "RNN": "RNN", "LSTM": "LSTM"}
FAMILY = {"Naive": "Referencia", "SARIMA": "Series de tiempo", "Holt-Winters": "Series de tiempo",
          "Regresión Lineal": "Machine Learning", "Random Forest": "Machine Learning",
          "RNN": "Deep Learning", "LSTM": "Deep Learning"}
EQUAL_TOL = 0.05        # % de diferencia de MAE frente a Naive por debajo del cual se considera "igual"


def published():
    global _PUB
    if _PUB is None:
        with open(_PUB_PATH, encoding="utf-8") as fh:
            _PUB = json.load(fh)
    return _PUB


def _rel(mae, naive_mae):
    """Diferencia porcentual del MAE frente al modelo ingenuo (negativo = mejor que Naive)."""
    if mae is None or not naive_mae:
        return None
    return (mae / naive_mae - 1.0) * 100.0


def _cell_class(rel, winner_verdict):
    if winner_verdict == "mejor que Naive":
        return "sig"
    if rel is None:
        return "na"
    if rel < -EQUAL_TOL:
        return "better"
    if rel <= EQUAL_TOL:
        return "equal"
    return "worse"


def _lectura(e, models, naive):
    """Una frase de lectura para el elemento, construida solo con hechos de la tabla."""
    top7 = [m["Modelo"] for m in models if m["rank7"] == 1]
    best6 = [m for m in models if m.get("rank6") == 1]
    parts = []
    if "Naive" in top7:
        others = [ABBR[m] for m in top7 if m != "Naive"]
        parts.append("El modelo ingenuo queda en primer lugar" + (f" (empatado con {', '.join(others)})" if others else "") + ".")
    else:
        parts.append(f"Primer lugar en el ranking con los 7 modelos: {', '.join(ABBR[m] for m in top7)}.")
    if best6:
        b = best6[0]
        rel = _rel(b["MAE"], naive["MAE"])
        rel_txt = ("igual que Naive" if rel is not None and abs(rel) <= EQUAL_TOL
                   else f"{rel:+.1f} % de MAE frente a Naive" if rel is not None else "")
        parts.append(f"Mejor de los seis propuestos: {', '.join(ABBR[m['Modelo']] for m in best6)} (MAE {b['MAE']:.4f}, {rel_txt}).")
    sig_better = [ABBR[m["Modelo"]] for m in models if m["veredicto"] == "mejor que Naive"]
    sig_worse = [ABBR[m["Modelo"]] for m in models if m["veredicto"] == "peor que Naive"]
    if sig_better:
        parts.append(f"Supera a Naive con significancia: {', '.join(sig_better)}.")
        for m in models:
            if m["veredicto"] != "mejor que Naive":
                continue
            ph, pb = m.get("p_holm"), m.get("p_holm_bank")
            if ph is not None and ph >= 0.05:
                parts.append(f"Con la corrección de Holm por las seis comparaciones del experimento "
                             f"(p ajustado = {ph:.3f}) esa ventaja deja de ser significativa.")
            elif ph is not None:
                parts.append(f"La ventaja se mantiene con la corrección de Holm dentro del experimento (p ajustado = {ph:.3f}, "
                             f"porque las otras comparaciones son extremas)"
                             + (f", pero no al considerar las pruebas del banco completo (p ajustado = {pb:.2f})." if pb is not None and pb >= 0.05 else "."))
    else:
        parts.append("Ningún modelo supera a Naive con significancia estadística.")
    if sig_worse:
        parts.append(f"Significativamente peores que Naive: {', '.join(sig_worse)}.")
    sarima = next((m for m in models if m["Modelo"] == "SARIMA"), None)
    if sarima and sarima["MAE"] == naive["MAE"] and "(0, 1, 0)" in e.get("sarima_order", ""):
        parts.append("auto_arima eligió (0,1,0): SARIMA es idéntico al paseo aleatorio.")
    return " ".join(parts)


def _build_context(pub):
    periods, tests = [], [{"T": T, "pct": pct} for T, pct in pub["tests"].items()]
    for P, per in pub["periods"].items():
        e1 = pub["elements"][f"{P},T1"]
        periods.append({"P": P, "key": per["key"], "label": per["label"], "n": e1["n"],
                        "fecha_inicio": e1["fecha_inicio"], "fecha_fin": e1["fecha_fin"]})

    heat, rank1, details = [], [], []
    dm_counts = {m: {"mejor que Naive": 0, "sin diferencia": 0, "peor que Naive": 0, "n/a": 0, "mejor (Holm)": 0}
                 for m in MODELS6}
    for per in periods:
        P = per["P"]
        cells, counts, elems = [], {m: 0 for m in MODELS6}, []
        for t in tests:
            el = f"{P},{t['T']}"
            e = pub["elements"][el]
            models = sorted(e["models"], key=lambda m: (m["rank7"], m["MAE"] if m["MAE"] is not None else 9e9))
            by = {m["Modelo"]: m for m in models}
            naive = by["Naive"]
            winners = [m for m in models if m.get("rank6") == 1]
            w = winners[0] if winners else None
            rel = _rel(w["MAE"], naive["MAE"]) if w else None
            cls = _cell_class(rel, w["veredicto"] if w else None)
            for m in models:
                if m["Modelo"] != "Naive":
                    dm_counts[m["Modelo"]][m["veredicto"]] += 1
                    if m["veredicto"] == "mejor que Naive" and m.get("p_holm") is not None and m["p_holm"] < 0.05:
                        dm_counts[m["Modelo"]]["mejor (Holm)"] += 1
                if m.get("rank6") == 1:
                    counts[m["Modelo"]] += 1
            cells.append({
                "el": el, "anchor": el.replace(",", "-"), "T": t["T"], "pct": t["pct"],
                "winners": [ABBR[m["Modelo"]] for m in winners],
                "mae": w["MAE"] if w else None, "naive_mae": naive["MAE"], "rel": rel, "cls": cls,
                "naive_first": naive["rank7"] == 1,
                "title": (f"{el}: historia de {per['label']}, {t['pct']} % de prueba (n = {e['n']}). "
                          f"Mejor de los seis: {', '.join(ABBR[m['Modelo']] for m in winners)} con MAE {w['MAE']:.4f}; "
                          f"Naive {naive['MAE']:.4f}." if w else el),
            })
            max_mae = max(m["MAE"] for m in models if m["MAE"] is not None)
            rows = []
            for m in models:
                r = _rel(m["MAE"], naive["MAE"])
                rows.append({**m, "abbr": ABBR[m["Modelo"]], "family": FAMILY[m["Modelo"]],
                             "bar": (m["MAE"] / max_mae * 100.0) if m["MAE"] is not None and max_mae else 0.0,
                             "rel": r, "is_naive": m["Modelo"] == "Naive"})
            elems.append({"el": el, "anchor": el.replace(",", "-"), "T": t["T"], "pct": t["pct"], "n": e["n"],
                          "n_train": e["n_train"], "n_test": e["n_test"], "fecha_inicio": e["fecha_inicio"],
                          "fecha_test": e["fecha_test"], "fecha_fin": e["fecha_fin"],
                          "sarima_order": e.get("sarima_order", ""), "rows": rows,
                          "lectura": _lectura(e, models, naive)})
        heat.append({**per, "cells": cells})
        rank1.append({**per, "counts": counts, "naive_first": sum(1 for c in cells if c["naive_first"])})
        details.append({**per, "elements": elems})

    rank1_total = {m: sum(r["counts"][m] for r in rank1) for m in MODELS6}
    span = {"inicio": min(p["fecha_inicio"] for p in periods), "fin": max(p["fecha_fin"] for p in periods)}
    summary = {
        "n_elements": pub["summary"]["n_elements"],
        "naive_first": sum(r["naive_first"] for r in rank1),
        "better": pub["summary"]["better_than_naive"],
        "better_holm": pub["summary"].get("better_holm", 0),
        "better_holm_bank": pub["summary"].get("better_holm_bank", 0),
        "n_tests": pub["summary"].get("n_tests", 0),
        "worse": pub["summary"]["worse_than_naive"],
    }
    return dict(pub=pub, periods=periods, tests=tests, heat=heat, rank1=rank1, rank1_total=rank1_total,
                dm_counts=dm_counts, details=details, span=span, summary=summary,
                models6=MODELS6, abbr=ABBR, anchor=pub["anchor"])


@bp.route("/banco")
def banco():
    global _CTX
    if _CTX is None:
        _CTX = _build_context(published())
    return render_template("banco.html", **_CTX)

══ ARCHIVO: app\routes\exports.py ════════════════════════════════════════════

"""Rutas de exportación: Excel y PDF."""

import io
from datetime import datetime

import matplotlib
matplotlib.use("Agg")                   # backend sin pantalla (seguro en servidor)
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from flask import Blueprint, send_file, request
from reportlab.lib import colors as rl_colors
from reportlab.lib.colors import HexColor
from reportlab.lib.enums import TA_CENTER
from reportlab.lib.pagesizes import A4
from reportlab.lib.styles import getSampleStyleSheet, ParagraphStyle
from reportlab.lib.units import cm
from reportlab.platypus import (
    Flowable, HRFlowable, Image as RLImage,
    Paragraph, SimpleDocTemplate, Spacer, Table, TableStyle,
)

from app import cache
from src.models.ranking import add_ranking

bp = Blueprint("exports", __name__)

matplotlib.rcParams.update({
    "font.family":        "DejaVu Sans",
    "axes.spines.top":    False,
    "axes.spines.right":  False,
    "figure.facecolor":   "white",
    "axes.facecolor":     "#F8FAFD",
})


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Helpers gráficos
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class _ColorDot(Flowable):
    """Círculo de color sólido para el semáforo en el PDF."""
    def __init__(self, hex_color, diameter=22):
        Flowable.__init__(self)
        self._color = HexColor(hex_color)
        self._d = diameter

    def wrap(self, *args):
        return self._d, self._d

    def draw(self):
        r = self._d / 2
        self.canv.setFillColor(self._color)
        self.canv.circle(r, r, r - 1, fill=1, stroke=0)


def _band_sigma(cached, model_name, pred_vals, test_vals):
    """σ de la banda del 95 %: errores de entrenamiento del modelo; si no existen, errores de prueba."""
    sigma = float(cached.get("extras", {}).get("sigma_train", {}).get(model_name, float("nan")))
    if np.isfinite(sigma) and sigma > 0:
        return sigma, "entrenamiento"
    return float(np.std(np.array(pred_vals) - np.array(test_vals))), "prueba"


def _coverage(cached, model_name):
    """Cobertura empírica de la banda del 95 % sobre el periodo de prueba."""
    pred_vals = [v for v in cached["predictions_dict"].get(model_name, []) if v is not None]
    test_vals = [v for v in cached["test_series"] if v is not None]
    if not pred_vals or len(pred_vals) != len(test_vals):
        return None
    sigma, source = _band_sigma(cached, model_name, pred_vals, test_vals)
    inside = np.abs(np.array(test_vals) - np.array(pred_vals)) <= 1.96 * sigma
    return {"pct": float(inside.mean() * 100), "n_in": int(inside.sum()), "n": int(len(inside)),
            "sigma": sigma, "source": source}


def _forecast_png(cached, best_model, w_cm=17.0, h_cm=7.0):
    """PNG con la serie completa + predicción del mejor modelo + IC 95%."""
    train_s = cached["train_series"]
    test_s  = cached["test_series"]
    pred_s  = cached["predictions_dict"].get(best_model, [])
    dates   = cached["dates"]

    y_train = [x if x is not None else float("nan") for x in train_s]
    y_test  = [x if x is not None else float("nan") for x in test_s]
    y_pred  = [x if x is not None else float("nan") for x in pred_s]
    x_all   = list(range(len(dates)))

    pred_vals = [v for v in pred_s  if v is not None]
    test_vals = [v for v in test_s  if v is not None]
    n_train   = sum(1 for v in train_s if v is not None)

    if pred_vals and len(pred_vals) == len(test_vals):
        sigma = _band_sigma(cached, best_model, pred_vals, test_vals)[0]
        ci_u = [float("nan")] * n_train + [v + 1.96 * sigma for v in pred_vals]
        ci_l = [float("nan")] * n_train + [v - 1.96 * sigma for v in pred_vals]
    else:
        ci_u = ci_l = [float("nan")] * len(dates)

    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(w_cm / 2.54, h_cm / 2.54))

    ax.plot(x_all, y_train, color="#003087", linewidth=1.2, label="Entrenamiento", zorder=2)
    ax.plot(x_all, y_test,  color="#27AE60", linewidth=1.6, label="Real (test)",   zorder=3)
    ax.plot(x_all, y_pred,  color="#C0392B", linewidth=1.4, linestyle="--",
            label=best_model, zorder=4)
    ax.fill_between(x_all, ci_l, ci_u, color="#C0392B", alpha=0.13, label="IC 95 %", zorder=1)

    n = len(dates)
    step = max(1, n // 8)
    ticks = list(range(0, n, step))
    ax.set_xticks(ticks)
    ax.set_xticklabels([dates[i] for i in ticks], rotation=35, ha="right", fontsize=6)
    ax.set_ylabel("MXN / USD", fontsize=8)
    ax.legend(fontsize=7, loc="upper left", framealpha=0.75)
    ax.grid(True, alpha=0.25, linewidth=0.5)
    ax.set_title(f"Pronóstico vs. Real — {best_model}  (IC 95 %)", fontsize=9,
                 color="#003087", fontweight="bold", pad=6)
    fig.tight_layout()

    buf = io.BytesIO()
    fig.savefig(buf, format="PNG", dpi=150, bbox_inches="tight")
    plt.close(fig)
    buf.seek(0)
    return buf


def _residuals_png(cached, best_model, w_cm=17.0, h_cm=5.5):
    """PNG con los residuales en el tiempo para el mejor modelo."""
    train_s = cached["train_series"]
    test_s  = cached["test_series"]
    pred_s  = cached["predictions_dict"].get(best_model, [])
    dates   = cached["dates"]

    test_vals = [v for v in test_s if v is not None]
    pred_vals = [v for v in pred_s if v is not None]
    n_train   = sum(1 for v in train_s if v is not None)
    dates_test = dates[n_train:]

    if not pred_vals or len(pred_vals) != len(test_vals):
        return None

    resid  = np.array(pred_vals) - np.array(test_vals)
    colors = ["#C0392B" if r > 0 else "#003087" for r in resid]
    mean_r = float(np.mean(resid))

    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(w_cm / 2.54, h_cm / 2.54))
    x = list(range(len(resid)))
    ax.bar(x, resid, color=colors, alpha=0.75, width=0.7, zorder=2)
    ax.axhline(0,      color="#C8A800", linewidth=1.2, linestyle="--", zorder=3)
    ax.axhline(mean_r, color="#555555", linewidth=0.8, linestyle=":",
               label=f"Media residual: {mean_r:.4f}", zorder=3)

    step = max(1, len(dates_test) // 6)
    ticks = list(range(0, len(dates_test), step))
    ax.set_xticks(ticks)
    ax.set_xticklabels([dates_test[i] for i in ticks], rotation=35, ha="right", fontsize=6)
    ax.set_ylabel("Residual  (pred − real)", fontsize=8)
    ax.legend(fontsize=7, framealpha=0.75)
    ax.grid(True, alpha=0.2, axis="y", linewidth=0.5)
    ax.set_title(f"Residuales en el tiempo — {best_model}", fontsize=9,
                 color="#003087", fontweight="bold", pad=6)
    fig.tight_layout()

    buf = io.BytesIO()
    fig.savefig(buf, format="PNG", dpi=150, bbox_inches="tight")
    plt.close(fig)
    buf.seek(0)
    return buf


def _semaforo_block(semaforo, avail_w, body_s):
    """Tabla-bloque con el semáforo de riesgo para el PDF."""
    if not semaforo:
        return []

    LEVELS = {
        "low":    ("#2ECC71", "#D5F5E3", "#1A7340"),
        "medium": ("#E67E22", "#FDEBD0", "#7D5A00"),
        "high":   ("#E74C3C", "#FADBD8", "#7B241C"),
    }
    dot_hex, bg_hex, txt_hex = LEVELS.get(semaforo["level"], ("#999999", "#F5F5F5", "#333333"))

    dot = _ColorDot(dot_hex, diameter=22)

    label_s = ParagraphStyle("SL", parent=body_s, fontName="Helvetica-Bold",
                              fontSize=9, textColor=HexColor(txt_hex), leading=13)
    info_s  = ParagraphStyle("SI", parent=body_s, fontSize=8,
                              textColor=HexColor(txt_hex), leading=12)

    content = Table([
        [Paragraph(f"{semaforo['label']}  —  Volatilidad anualizada: {semaforo['annual_vol']} %", label_s)],
        [Spacer(1, 3)],
        [Paragraph(semaforo["desc"], info_s)],
        [Spacer(1, 3)],
        [Paragraph(f"<b>Recomendación:</b> {semaforo['rec']}", info_s)],
    ], colWidths=[avail_w - 42])
    content.setStyle(TableStyle([
        ("TOPPADDING",    (0, 0), (-1, -1), 0),
        ("BOTTOMPADDING", (0, 0), (-1, -1), 0),
        ("LEFTPADDING",   (0, 0), (-1, -1), 0),
        ("RIGHTPADDING",  (0, 0), (-1, -1), 0),
    ]))

    outer = Table([[dot, content]], colWidths=[32, avail_w - 32])
    outer.setStyle(TableStyle([
        ("BACKGROUND",    (0, 0), (-1, -1), HexColor(bg_hex)),
        ("VALIGN",        (0, 0), (-1, -1), "MIDDLE"),
        ("TOPPADDING",    (0, 0), (-1, -1), 10),
        ("BOTTOMPADDING", (0, 0), (-1, -1), 10),
        ("LEFTPADDING",   (0, 0), (-1, -1), 10),
        ("RIGHTPADDING",  (0, 0), (-1, -1), 10),
    ]))
    return [outer]


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Excel
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

@bp.route("/download_excel", methods=["POST"])
def download_excel():
    cache_key  = request.form.get("cache_key", "")
    model_name = request.form.get("model_name", "modelo")
    cached = cache.get(cache_key) if cache_key else None

    if not cached:
        return "Sesión expirada o inválida. Vuelve a la página principal y consulta de nuevo.", 400

    train_series = cached["train_series"]
    test_series  = cached["test_series"]
    pred_series  = cached["predictions_dict"].get(model_name, [])
    dates        = cached["dates"]

    df = pd.DataFrame({
        "Date":          dates,
        "Entrenamiento": train_series,
        "Real (test)":   test_series,
        "Pronóstico":    pred_series,
    })

    output = io.BytesIO()
    with pd.ExcelWriter(output, engine="xlsxwriter") as writer:
        df.to_excel(writer, index=False, sheet_name="Datos")
        workbook  = writer.book
        worksheet = writer.sheets["Datos"]
        chart = workbook.add_chart({"type": "line"})
        for idx, col in enumerate(["Entrenamiento", "Real (test)", "Pronóstico"]):
            chart.add_series({
                "name":       col,
                "categories": ["Datos", 1, 0, len(df), 0],
                "values":     ["Datos", 1, idx + 1, len(df), idx + 1],
            })
        worksheet.insert_chart("F2", chart)
    output.seek(0)

    return send_file(
        output,
        as_attachment=True,
        download_name=f"{model_name}_resultado.xlsx",
        mimetype="application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet",
    )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# PDF
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

@bp.route("/download_pdf", methods=["POST"])
def download_pdf():
    cache_key = request.form.get("cache_key", "")
    cached = cache.get(cache_key) if cache_key else None

    if not cached:
        return "Sesión expirada o inválida. Vuelve a la página principal y consulta de nuevo.", 400

    metrics_df = cached["metrics_df"].copy()
    fc_values  = cached["forecast_values"]
    semaforo   = cached.get("semaforo")

    metrics_df, best_idx = add_ranking(metrics_df)
    best_model = str(metrics_df.loc[best_idx, "Modelo"])

    # ── Colores corporativos ────────────────────────────────────────────────
    UNAM_BLUE  = HexColor("#003087")
    UNAM_GOLD  = HexColor("#C8A800")
    UNAM_LIGHT = HexColor("#F4F6FA")
    WHITE      = rl_colors.white
    DARK       = HexColor("#0D1B3E")
    GRAY       = HexColor("#4A5568")
    GOLD_TINT  = HexColor("#FFFBE6")

    # ── Documento ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    buf = io.BytesIO()
    doc = SimpleDocTemplate(
        buf, pagesize=A4,
        leftMargin=2*cm, rightMargin=2*cm, topMargin=1.5*cm, bottomMargin=2*cm,
    )
    AVAIL_W = 17 * cm
    base = getSampleStyleSheet()

    def ps(name, **kw):
        return ParagraphStyle(name, parent=base["Normal"], **kw)

    title_s   = ps("T",  fontName="Helvetica-Bold", fontSize=15, textColor=WHITE,     alignment=TA_CENTER, spaceAfter=2)
    sub_s     = ps("Su", fontName="Helvetica",      fontSize=8,  textColor=HexColor("#C8D8FF"), alignment=TA_CENTER, spaceAfter=2)
    section_s = ps("H",  fontName="Helvetica-Bold", fontSize=11, textColor=UNAM_BLUE, spaceBefore=14, spaceAfter=6)
    body_s    = ps("B",  fontName="Helvetica",      fontSize=9,  textColor=DARK,      leading=14)
    small_s   = ps("Sm", fontName="Helvetica",      fontSize=7.5,textColor=GRAY,      leading=11)
    footer_s  = ps("F",  fontName="Helvetica",      fontSize=7,  textColor=GRAY,      alignment=TA_CENTER)

    story = []

    # ── Encabezado ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    hdr = Table([
        [Paragraph("Universidad Nacional Autónoma de México · Facultad de Ingeniería", sub_s)],
        [Paragraph("Reporte de Pronóstico — Tipo de Cambio USD/MXN", title_s)],
        [Paragraph("Modelos de Series de Tiempo, Machine Learning &amp; Deep Learning · Tesis 2026", sub_s)],
    ], colWidths=[AVAIL_W])
    hdr.setStyle(TableStyle([
        ("BACKGROUND",    (0,0), (-1,-1), UNAM_BLUE),
        ("TOPPADDING",    (0,0), (-1,-1), 8),
        ("BOTTOMPADDING", (0,0), (-1,-1), 8),
        ("LEFTPADDING",   (0,0), (-1,-1), 14),
        ("RIGHTPADDING",  (0,0), (-1,-1), 14),
    ]))
    story.append(hdr)
    story.append(HRFlowable(width="100%", thickness=4, color=UNAM_GOLD, spaceAfter=8))

    now_str = datetime.now().strftime("%d/%m/%Y  %H:%M hrs")
    meta = Table([
        ["Fecha de generación:", now_str],
        ["Autor:", "Ing. Ricardo Javier Martínez Suástegui"],
        ["Mejor modelo:", best_model],
    ], colWidths=[4.5*cm, AVAIL_W - 4.5*cm])
    meta.setStyle(TableStyle([
        ("FONT",          (0,0), (0,-1), "Helvetica-Bold", 8),
        ("FONT",          (1,0), (1,-1), "Helvetica",      8),
        ("TEXTCOLOR",     (0,0), (0,-1), UNAM_BLUE),
        ("TEXTCOLOR",     (1,0), (1,-1), DARK),
        ("TOPPADDING",    (0,0), (-1,-1), 3),
        ("BOTTOMPADDING", (0,0), (-1,-1), 3),
    ]))
    story.append(meta)
    story.append(Spacer(1, 0.2*cm))
    story.append(HRFlowable(width="100%", thickness=1, color=UNAM_GOLD, spaceAfter=6))

    # ── Sección 1: Métricas ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
    story.append(Paragraph("1. Métricas Comparativas de Modelos", section_s))
    def _num(v, fmt):
        return fmt.format(v) if v is not None and not pd.isna(v) else "—"

    m_rows = [["Modelo", "MAE", "RMSE", "MAPE", "R²", "DM p", "p aj.", "Veredicto", "Rank"]]
    for _, row in metrics_df.iterrows():
        m_rows.append([
            str(row["Modelo"]),
            _num(row["MAE"], "{:.4f}"), _num(row["RMSE"], "{:.4f}"),
            _num(row["MAPE"], "{:.4f}"), _num(row["R^2"], "{:.4f}"),
            _num(row.get("DM p-valor"), "{:.4f}"), _num(row.get("DM p ajust."), "{:.4f}"),
            str(row.get("Veredicto", "")), str(int(row["Rank"])),
        ])

    col_w = [3.0*cm, 1.8*cm, 1.8*cm, 1.8*cm, 1.8*cm, 1.5*cm, 1.5*cm, 2.7*cm, 1.1*cm]
    m_tbl = Table(m_rows, colWidths=col_w)
    ts = [
        ("BACKGROUND",    (0,0), (-1,0),  UNAM_BLUE),
        ("TEXTCOLOR",     (0,0), (-1,0),  WHITE),
        ("FONT",          (0,0), (-1,0),  "Helvetica-Bold", 8),
        ("FONT",          (0,1), (-1,-1), "Helvetica",      8),
        ("TEXTCOLOR",     (0,1), (-1,-1), DARK),
        ("ALIGN",         (0,0), (-1,-1), "CENTER"),
        ("ALIGN",         (0,1), (0,-1),  "LEFT"),
        ("GRID",          (0,0), (-1,-1), 0.4, HexColor("#D6DCE8")),
        ("TOPPADDING",    (0,0), (-1,-1), 6),
        ("BOTTOMPADDING", (0,0), (-1,-1), 6),
        ("LEFTPADDING",   (0,0), (-1,-1), 8),
        ("RIGHTPADDING",  (0,0), (-1,-1), 8),
    ]
    for r in range(1, len(m_rows)):
        if r % 2 == 0:
            ts.append(("BACKGROUND", (0, r), (-1, r), UNAM_LIGHT))
    best_row = list(metrics_df.index).index(best_idx) + 1
    ts += [
        ("BACKGROUND", (0, best_row), (-1, best_row), GOLD_TINT),
        ("TEXTCOLOR",  (0, best_row), (-1, best_row), HexColor("#7A6000")),
        ("FONT",       (0, best_row), (-1, best_row), "Helvetica-Bold", 8),
        ("LINEBEFORE", (0, best_row), (0,  best_row), 4, UNAM_GOLD),
    ]
    m_tbl.setStyle(TableStyle(ts))
    story.append(m_tbl)
    story.append(Spacer(1, 0.15*cm))
    story.append(Paragraph(
        f"Mejor modelo: <b>{best_model}</b> — menor puntuación compuesta en MAE, RMSE, MAPE y R². "
        "Evaluación a un paso adelante; DM p = p-valor de la prueba de Diebold-Mariano frente al modelo ingenuo (Naive); "
        "p aj. = el mismo p-valor con la corrección de Holm por las seis comparaciones simultáneas.",
        small_s,
    ))

    # ── Sección 2: Semáforo de riesgo ───────────────────────────────────────
    story.append(Paragraph("2. Semáforo de Riesgo Cambiario", section_s))
    story.extend(_semaforo_block(semaforo, AVAIL_W, body_s))

    # ── Sección 3: Gráfica del mejor modelo (con IC 95%) ────────────────────
    story.append(Paragraph(f"3. Pronóstico del Mejor Modelo — {best_model}", section_s))
    cov = _coverage(cached, best_model)
    cov_txt = ""
    if cov:
        cov_txt = (f" Cobertura empírica en prueba: {cov['pct']:.1f} % ({cov['n_in']} de {cov['n']} valores reales "
                   f"dentro de la banda; nominal 95 %; sigma = {cov['sigma']:.4f}, errores de {cov['source']}).")
    story.append(Paragraph(
        "Serie histórica completa con la predicción del modelo ganador y su intervalo de confianza al 95 %. "
        "La banda sombreada se obtiene como ±1.96 desviaciones estándar de los errores de entrenamiento a un paso "
        "del modelo, de modo que la cobertura observada en prueba es una verificación honesta." + cov_txt,
        body_s,
    ))
    story.append(Spacer(1, 0.2*cm))
    try:
        fc_buf = _forecast_png(cached, best_model, w_cm=17.0, h_cm=7.0)
        story.append(RLImage(fc_buf, width=AVAIL_W, height=7*cm))
    except Exception:
        story.append(Paragraph("(No fue posible generar la gráfica.)", small_s))

    # ── Sección 4: Residuales en el tiempo ──────────────────────────────────
    story.append(Paragraph(f"4. Análisis de Residuales — {best_model}", section_s))
    story.append(Paragraph(
        "Diferencia entre la predicción y el valor real en cada punto del período de prueba. "
        "Barras rojas indican sobreestimación; barras azules indican subestimación. "
        "La línea dorada marca el cero; la punteada gris indica la media del error.",
        body_s,
    ))
    story.append(Spacer(1, 0.2*cm))
    try:
        res_buf = _residuals_png(cached, best_model, w_cm=17.0, h_cm=5.5)
        if res_buf:
            story.append(RLImage(res_buf, width=AVAIL_W, height=5.5*cm))
        else:
            story.append(Paragraph("(Datos insuficientes para graficar residuales.)", small_s))
    except Exception:
        story.append(Paragraph("(No fue posible generar la gráfica de residuales.)", small_s))

    # ── Sección 5: Valores pronosticados ────────────────────────────────────
    story.append(Paragraph("5. Valores Pronosticados", section_s))
    story.append(Paragraph(
        "Estimación de los próximos puntos del tipo de cambio USD/MXN según cada modelo:", body_s,
    ))
    story.append(Spacer(1, 0.2*cm))

    cell_s     = ps("C",  fontName="Helvetica",      fontSize=9, textColor=DARK,  leading=13, wordWrap="CJK")
    cell_hdr_s = ps("Ch", fontName="Helvetica-Bold", fontSize=8, textColor=WHITE, leading=12)

    fc_rows = [[Paragraph("Modelo", cell_hdr_s), Paragraph("Pronóstico (MXN/USD)", cell_hdr_s)]]
    for model, vals in fc_values.items():
        try:
            val_str = "    ".join(f"{v:.4f}" for v in vals if v is not None)
        except TypeError:
            val_str = f"{vals:.4f}" if vals is not None else "—"
        fc_rows.append([Paragraph(model, cell_s), Paragraph(val_str, cell_s)])

    fc_tbl = Table(fc_rows, colWidths=[4.5*cm, AVAIL_W - 4.5*cm])
    fts = [
        ("BACKGROUND",    (0,0), (-1,0),  UNAM_BLUE),
        ("ALIGN",         (0,0), (-1,-1), "LEFT"),
        ("VALIGN",        (0,0), (-1,-1), "TOP"),
        ("GRID",          (0,0), (-1,-1), 0.4, HexColor("#D6DCE8")),
        ("TOPPADDING",    (0,0), (-1,-1), 6),
        ("BOTTOMPADDING", (0,0), (-1,-1), 6),
        ("LEFTPADDING",   (0,0), (-1,-1), 8),
        ("RIGHTPADDING",  (0,0), (-1,-1), 8),
    ]
    for r in range(1, len(fc_rows)):
        if r % 2 == 0:
            fts.append(("BACKGROUND", (0, r), (-1, r), UNAM_LIGHT))
    fc_tbl.setStyle(TableStyle(fts))
    story.append(fc_tbl)

    # ── Sección 6: Nota de gestión de riesgo ────────────────────────────────
    story.append(Paragraph("6. Nota de Gestión de Riesgo Cambiario", section_s))
    for item in [
        "Los pronósticos son <b>estimaciones estadísticas</b> basadas en datos históricos, no garantías de comportamiento futuro.",
        "El tipo de cambio USD/MXN está sujeto a factores macroeconómicos, geopolíticos y de mercado que pueden generar desviaciones significativas.",
        "Para decisiones de <b>cobertura cambiaria</b> (forwards, opciones, swaps), se recomienda consultar con un especialista financiero.",
        "Los <b>intervalos de confianza al 95 %</b> del mejor modelo se muestran en la sección 3 de este reporte y en la vista de gráfica de la plataforma web.",
    ]:
        story.append(Paragraph(f"• {item}", body_s))
        story.append(Spacer(1, 0.1*cm))

    # ── Pie de página ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    story.append(Spacer(1, 0.4*cm))
    story.append(HRFlowable(width="100%", thickness=1, color=UNAM_GOLD, spaceBefore=4))
    story.append(Paragraph(
        "© 2026  Ing. Ricardo Javier Martínez Suástegui  —  "
        "Universidad Nacional Autónoma de México · Facultad de Ingeniería · Tesis de Licenciatura",
        footer_s,
    ))

    doc.build(story)
    buf.seek(0)

    filename = f"Reporte_USDMXN_{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M')}.pdf"
    return send_file(buf, as_attachment=True, download_name=filename, mimetype="application/pdf")

══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

══ ARCHIVO: app\routes\main.py ═══════════════════════════════════════════════

"""Rutas principales: dashboard, gráfica, residuales, estacionalidad, forecast vs real."""

from datetime import datetime

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from dateutil.relativedelta import relativedelta
from flask import Blueprint, render_template, request

from app import cache
from src.data.fetch import get_usdmxn
from src.features.build import compute_risk
from src.models.evaluation import ljung_box_p
from src.models.pipeline import train_and_evaluate
from src.models.ranking import add_ranking

bp = Blueprint("main", __name__)

VALID_PERIODS = {"3mo", "6mo", "1y", "2y", "5y", "10y", "1mo", "5d", "ytd", "max"}
PERIOD_LABELS = {
    "3mo": "3 meses (corto plazo)",
    "6mo": "6 meses (corto plazo)",
    "1y":  "1 año (mediano plazo)",
    "2y":  "2 años (mediano plazo)",
    "5y":  "5 años (largo plazo)",
    "10y": "10 años (largo plazo)",
    "1mo": "1 mes",
    "5d":  "5 días",
    "ytd": "Año en curso (YTD)",
    "max": "Máximo histórico",
}


def _render_index(**kwargs):
    defaults = dict(
        metrics_table=None, forecast_values=None, predictions_dict=None,
        display_period=None, display_test_percent=None, cache_key=None,
        error=None, selected_period="1y", selected_test_percent=20,
        query_mode="period", selected_start_date="", selected_end_date="",
        selected_anchor_date="", selected_anchor_period="1y",
        semaforo=None, direction_rows=None,
    )
    defaults.update(kwargs)
    return render_template("index.html", **defaults)


def _direction_rows(direction, order):
    """Filas de acierto de dirección (prueba de Pesaran-Timmermann) en el orden del ranking."""
    rows = []
    for name in order:
        d = direction.get(name) or {}
        hit, n, pt, p = d.get("hit"), d.get("n", 0), d.get("pt"), d.get("p")
        if name == "Naive" or hit is None or not np.isfinite(hit):
            rows.append({"Modelo": name, "hit": "—", "n": n, "pt": "—", "p": "—",
                         "verdict": "sin dirección: pronostica 'sin cambio'" if name == "Naive" else "sin dirección definida"})
            continue
        if p is not None and np.isfinite(p):
            verdict = "acierta más que el azar" if p < 0.05 else "no distinguible del azar"
        else:
            verdict = "pronostica siempre la misma dirección; la prueba no aplica"
        rows.append({"Modelo": name, "hit": f"{hit * 100:.1f} %", "n": n,
                     "pt": f"{pt:.2f}" if pt is not None and np.isfinite(pt) else "—",
                     "p": f"{p:.3f}" if p is not None and np.isfinite(p) else "—", "verdict": verdict})
    return rows


def _band_sigma(cached, model_name, pred_values, test_values):
    """σ de la banda del 95 %: errores de entrenamiento del modelo; si no existen, errores de prueba."""
    sigma = float(cached.get("extras", {}).get("sigma_train", {}).get(model_name, float("nan")))
    if np.isfinite(sigma) and sigma > 0:
        return sigma, "entrenamiento"
    return float(np.std(np.array(pred_values) - np.array(test_values))), "prueba"


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Ruta principal
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

@bp.route("/", methods=["GET", "POST"])
def index():
    if request.method != "POST":
        return _render_index()

    query_mode = request.form.get("query_mode", "period")

    try:
        test_percent = float(request.form.get("test_percent", 20))
        if not (1 <= test_percent <= 80):
            raise ValueError()
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
        return _render_index(error="El porcentaje de testing debe estar entre 1 y 80."), 400

    # Estado ancla — solo se usa en modo "anchor"; valores neutros para los otros modos
    anchor_date_str = ""
    anchor_period   = "1y"

    if query_mode == "period":
        selected_period = request.form.get("period_select", "")
        if selected_period not in VALID_PERIODS:
            return _render_index(error="Periodo inválido seleccionado."), 400
        start_date = end_date = None

    elif query_mode == "custom":
        selected_period = None
        start_date = request.form.get("start_date", "").strip()
        end_date   = request.form.get("end_date",   "").strip()
        try:
            dt_start = datetime.strptime(start_date, "%Y-%m-%d")
            dt_end   = datetime.strptime(end_date,   "%Y-%m-%d")
            if dt_start >= dt_end:
                raise ValueError("La fecha de inicio debe ser anterior a la fecha fin.")
            if dt_end > datetime.now():
                raise ValueError("La fecha fin no puede ser futura.")
        except (ValueError, TypeError) as exc:
            return _render_index(
                error=f"Fechas inválidas: {exc}", query_mode="custom",
                selected_start_date=start_date, selected_end_date=end_date,
                selected_test_percent=test_percent,
            ), 400

    elif query_mode == "anchor":
        selected_period = None
        anchor_date_str = request.form.get("anchor_date", "").strip()
        anchor_period   = request.form.get("anchor_period", "").strip()
        if anchor_period not in VALID_PERIODS:
            return _render_index(
                error="Periodo inválido en modo fecha ancla.", query_mode="anchor",
                selected_anchor_date=anchor_date_str, selected_anchor_period=anchor_period,
                selected_test_percent=test_percent,
            ), 400
        try:
            dt_anchor = datetime.strptime(anchor_date_str, "%Y-%m-%d")
            if dt_anchor > datetime.now():
                raise ValueError("La fecha de referencia no puede ser futura.")
            if dt_anchor < datetime(1993, 1, 1):
                raise ValueError("La fecha de referencia mínima disponible es el 01/01/1993.")

            _OFFSETS = {
                "5d":  relativedelta(days=5),
                "1mo": relativedelta(months=1),
                "3mo": relativedelta(months=3),
                "6mo": relativedelta(months=6),
                "1y":  relativedelta(years=1),
                "2y":  relativedelta(years=2),
                "5y":  relativedelta(years=5),
                "10y": relativedelta(years=10),
            }
            if anchor_period == "ytd":
                dt_start = datetime(dt_anchor.year, 1, 1)
            elif anchor_period == "max":
                dt_start = datetime(1993, 1, 1)
            else:
                dt_start = dt_anchor - _OFFSETS[anchor_period]

            if dt_start >= dt_anchor:
                raise ValueError(
                    f"El periodo '{PERIOD_LABELS.get(anchor_period, anchor_period)}' no genera "
                    "un rango válido desde la fecha de referencia indicada."
                )
            start_date = dt_start.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
            end_date   = anchor_date_str
        except (ValueError, TypeError) as exc:
            return _render_index(
                error=f"Fecha de referencia inválida: {exc}", query_mode="anchor",
                selected_anchor_date=anchor_date_str, selected_anchor_period=anchor_period,
                selected_test_percent=test_percent,
            ), 400

    else:
        return _render_index(error="Modo de consulta inválido."), 400

    today = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d")
    if query_mode == "period":
        cache_key = f"v2_{today}_period_{selected_period}_{int(test_percent)}"
    elif query_mode == "custom":
        cache_key = f"v2_{today}_custom_{start_date}_{end_date}_{int(test_percent)}"
    else:
        cache_key = f"v2_{today}_anchor_{anchor_date_str}_{anchor_period}_{int(test_percent)}"

    cached = cache.get(cache_key)
    if cached is None:
        df = get_usdmxn(period=selected_period, start_date=start_date, end_date=end_date)
        if df is None or len(df) < 10:
            return _render_index(
                error="No se obtuvieron suficientes datos. Prueba con otro periodo o rango de fechas.",
                query_mode=query_mode, selected_period=selected_period,
                selected_start_date=start_date or "", selected_end_date=end_date or "",
                selected_anchor_date=anchor_date_str, selected_anchor_period=anchor_period,
                selected_test_percent=test_percent,
            ), 400

        test_size = max(1, int(len(df) * test_percent / 100))
        metrics_df, forecast_values, train_series, test_series, predictions_dict, extras = (
            train_and_evaluate(df, forecast_steps=test_size, test_size=test_size)
        )
        dates = df["Date"].dt.strftime("%Y-%m-%d").tolist()
        semaforo = compute_risk(df["Close"].values)

        cached = {
            "metrics_df": metrics_df, "forecast_values": forecast_values,
            "train_series": train_series, "test_series": test_series,
            "predictions_dict": predictions_dict, "dates": dates, "semaforo": semaforo,
            "extras": extras,
        }
        cache.set(cache_key, cached)

    # Tabla de métricas con ranking
    metrics_df, best_idx = add_ranking(cached["metrics_df"])

    format_dict = {"MAE": "{:.4f}", "RMSE": "{:.4f}", "MAPE": "{:.4f}", "R^2": "{:.4f}",
                   "DM": "{:.3f}", "DM p-valor": "{:.4f}", "DM p ajust.": "{:.4f}", "Rank": "{:.0f}"}

    def highlight_best(row):
        if row.name == best_idx:
            return [
                "background: linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(200,168,0,0.18), rgba(200,168,0,0.08));"
                "border-left: 4px solid #C8A800; font-weight: 700; color: #7A6000;"
            ] * len(row)
        return [""] * len(row)

    metrics_table = (
        metrics_df.style
        .apply(highlight_best, axis=1)
        .format(format_dict, na_rep="—")
        .hide(axis="index")
        .set_table_styles([
            {"selector": "",           "props": [("border-collapse", "collapse"), ("width", "100%")]},
            {"selector": "thead tr",   "props": [("background", "linear-gradient(90deg,#003087,#005EB8)")]},
            {"selector": "th",         "props": [("color", "#FFFFFF"), ("font-weight", "700"),
                                                  ("font-size", "0.75rem"), ("letter-spacing", "0.08em"),
                                                  ("text-transform", "uppercase"), ("padding", "12px 14px"),
                                                  ("text-align", "center"), ("border", "none")]},
            {"selector": "td",         "props": [("padding", "10px 14px"), ("text-align", "center"),
                                                  ("border-bottom", "1px solid #D6DCE8"),
                                                  ("vertical-align", "middle")]},
            {"selector": "tbody tr:nth-child(even)", "props": [("background-color", "rgba(0,48,135,0.03)")]},
            {"selector": "tbody tr:hover",           "props": [("background-color", "rgba(0,48,135,0.07)")]},
        ])
        .to_html(classes="w-100", table_id="metrics-table")
    )

    def format_forecast(vals):
        try:
            return ", ".join(f"{v:.2f}" for v in vals if v is not None)
        except TypeError:
            return f"{vals:.2f}" if vals is not None else ""

    formatted_forecasts = {m: format_forecast(v) for m, v in cached["forecast_values"].items()}
    if query_mode == "period":
        display_period = PERIOD_LABELS.get(selected_period, selected_period)
    elif query_mode == "anchor":
        display_period = (
            f"{start_date} → {end_date}"
            f"  ({PERIOD_LABELS.get(anchor_period, anchor_period)})"
        )
    else:
        display_period = f"{start_date} → {end_date}"

    return _render_index(
        metrics_table=metrics_table, forecast_values=formatted_forecasts,
        predictions_dict=cached["predictions_dict"], display_period=display_period,
        display_test_percent=test_percent, cache_key=cache_key, query_mode=query_mode,
        selected_period=selected_period, selected_start_date=start_date or "",
        selected_end_date=end_date or "", selected_test_percent=test_percent,
        selected_anchor_date=anchor_date_str, selected_anchor_period=anchor_period,
        semaforo=cached.get("semaforo"),
        direction_rows=_direction_rows(cached.get("extras", {}).get("direction", {}), metrics_df["Modelo"].tolist()),
    )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Ruta de gráfica
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

@bp.route("/plot", methods=["POST"])
def plot():
    cache_key  = request.form.get("cache_key", "")
    model_name = request.form.get("model_choice", "")
    cached = cache.get(cache_key) if cache_key else None

    if not cached:
        return "Sesión expirada o inválida. Vuelve a la página principal y consulta de nuevo.", 400

    train_series = cached["train_series"]
    test_series  = cached["test_series"]
    pred_series  = cached["predictions_dict"].get(model_name, [])
    dates        = cached["dates"]

    n_train      = sum(1 for x in train_series if x is not None)
    pred_values  = [x for x in pred_series  if x is not None]
    test_values  = [x for x in test_series  if x is not None]

    coverage = None
    if pred_values and test_values and len(pred_values) == len(test_values):
        sigma, sigma_source = _band_sigma(cached, model_name, pred_values, test_values)
        ci_upper = [None] * n_train + [round(v + 1.96 * sigma, 4) for v in pred_values]
        ci_lower = [None] * n_train + [round(v - 1.96 * sigma, 4) for v in pred_values]
        inside = np.abs(np.array(test_values) - np.array(pred_values)) <= 1.96 * sigma
        pct = float(inside.mean() * 100)
        if pct < 90:
            reading = ("La banda es más estrecha de lo que debería: el modelo se equivoca más en prueba que en "
                       "entrenamiento, señal de sobreajuste.")
        elif pct > 98.5:
            reading = "La banda es más ancha de lo necesario: los errores de prueba fueron menores que los de entrenamiento."
        else:
            reading = "La banda es consistente con su nivel nominal del 95 %."
        coverage = {"pct": round(pct, 1), "n_in": int(inside.sum()), "n": int(len(inside)),
                    "sigma": round(sigma, 4), "source": sigma_source, "reading": reading}
    else:
        ci_upper = [None] * len(pred_series)
        ci_lower = [None] * len(pred_series)

    def fmt(series):
        return ", ".join(f"{x:.2f}" for x in series if x is not None)

    return render_template(
        "plot.html",
        model_name=model_name, train_series=train_series, test_series=test_series,
        pred_series=pred_series, ci_upper=ci_upper, ci_lower=ci_lower, dates=dates,
        train_display=fmt(train_series), test_display=fmt(test_series),
        pred_display=fmt(pred_series), cache_key=cache_key, coverage=coverage,
    )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Análisis de residuales
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

@bp.route("/residuals", methods=["POST"])
def residuals():
    cache_key    = request.form.get("cache_key", "")
    model_filter = request.form.get("model_choice", "").strip()
    cached = cache.get(cache_key) if cache_key else None

    if not cached:
        return "Sesión expirada o inválida. Vuelve a la página principal y consulta de nuevo.", 400

    train_series     = cached["train_series"]
    test_series      = cached["test_series"]
    predictions_dict = cached["predictions_dict"]
    dates            = cached["dates"]

    n_train     = sum(1 for x in train_series if x is not None)
    test_values = [x for x in test_series if x is not None]
    dates_test  = dates[n_train:]

    residuals_data = {}
    residuals_stats = {}
    hist_data = {}
    interpretations = {}

    for model_name, pred_series in predictions_dict.items():
        if model_filter and model_name != model_filter:
            continue
        pred_values = [x for x in pred_series if x is not None]
        if not pred_values or len(pred_values) != len(test_values):
            continue

        resid = np.array(pred_values, dtype=float) - np.array(test_values, dtype=float)
        residuals_data[model_name] = [round(float(r), 4) for r in resid]

        mean_r = float(np.mean(resid))
        std_r  = float(np.std(resid))
        mae_r  = float(np.mean(np.abs(resid)))
        residuals_stats[model_name] = {
            "mean": round(mean_r, 4), "std": round(std_r, 4), "mae": round(mae_r, 4),
            "max": round(float(np.max(resid)), 4), "min": round(float(np.min(resid)), 4),
        }

        counts, edges = np.histogram(resid, bins=10)
        hist_data[model_name] = {
            "counts": counts.tolist(),
            "labels": [f"{float(edges[i]):.3f}" for i in range(len(edges) - 1)],
        }

        if abs(mean_r) < 0.05:
            bias_txt = "La media de residuales es cercana a cero, por lo que el modelo es prácticamente insesgado."
        elif mean_r > 0:
            bias_txt = f"La media positiva ({mean_r:.4f}) indica que el modelo tiende a <strong>sobreestimar</strong> el tipo de cambio."
        else:
            bias_txt = f"La media negativa ({mean_r:.4f}) indica que el modelo tiende a <strong>subestimar</strong> el tipo de cambio."

        cv = std_r / mae_r if mae_r > 0 else 0
        if cv < 0.6:
            var_txt = "La baja variabilidad de los errores refleja predicciones consistentes."
        elif cv < 1.1:
            var_txt = "Se observa variabilidad moderada en los errores."
        else:
            var_txt = "La alta variabilidad sugiere que el modelo presenta dificultades para capturar ciertos patrones de la serie."

        lb_p, lb_lags = ljung_box_p(resid, lags=10)
        residuals_stats[model_name]["lb_p"] = f"{lb_p:.3f}" if np.isfinite(lb_p) else "—"
        residuals_stats[model_name]["lb_lags"] = lb_lags
        if not np.isfinite(lb_p):
            lb_txt = ""
        elif lb_p >= 0.05:
            lb_txt = (f" Prueba de Ljung-Box (p = {lb_p:.3f}, {lb_lags} rezagos): los residuales <strong>no muestran "
                      "autocorrelación</strong>; lo que queda es ruido sin estructura que otro modelo pudiera aprovechar.")
        else:
            lb_txt = (f" Prueba de Ljung-Box (p = {lb_p:.3f}, {lb_lags} rezagos): los residuales <strong>conservan "
                      "autocorrelación</strong>; quedan patrones que el modelo no capturó.")
        interpretations[model_name] = f"{bias_txt} {var_txt}{lb_txt}"

    return render_template(
        "residuals.html",
        residuals_data=residuals_data, residuals_stats=residuals_stats,
        hist_data=hist_data, interpretations=interpretations,
        dates_test=dates_test, cache_key=cache_key,
        stationarity=cached.get("extras", {}).get("stationarity"),
    )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Historial de pronósticos vs. realidad
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

@bp.route("/forecast_vs_real", methods=["POST"])
def forecast_vs_real():
    cache_key = request.form.get("cache_key", "")
    cached = cache.get(cache_key) if cache_key else None

    if not cached:
        return "Sesión expirada o inválida. Vuelve a la página principal y consulta de nuevo.", 400

    train_series     = cached["train_series"]
    test_series      = cached["test_series"]
    predictions_dict = cached["predictions_dict"]
    dates            = cached["dates"]

    train_values = [float(x) for x in train_series if x is not None]
    n_train      = len(train_values)
    test_values  = [float(x) for x in test_series  if x is not None]
    n_test       = len(test_values)
    dates_test   = dates[len(dates) - n_test:]
    dates_all    = dates

    model_preds = {}
    for model, pred_series in predictions_dict.items():
        vals = [float(x) for x in pred_series if x is not None]
        if len(vals) == n_test:
            model_preds[model] = vals

    accuracy_stats = {}
    for model, preds in model_preds.items():
        errs_pct = [abs(p - r) / max(abs(r), 1e-10) * 100 for p, r in zip(preds, test_values)]
        mae = float(np.mean(np.abs(np.array(preds) - np.array(test_values))))
        accuracy_stats[model] = {
            "mae":         round(mae, 4),
            "within_1pct": round(sum(1 for e in errs_pct if e <= 1) / len(errs_pct) * 100, 1),
            "within_2pct": round(sum(1 for e in errs_pct if e <= 2) / len(errs_pct) * 100, 1),
            "within_5pct": round(sum(1 for e in errs_pct if e <= 5) / len(errs_pct) * 100, 1),
        }

    best_model = min(accuracy_stats, key=lambda m: accuracy_stats[m]["mae"]) if accuracy_stats else ""

    err_pct_matrix = {
        model: [round(abs(p - r) / max(abs(r), 1e-10) * 100, 3) for p, r in zip(preds, test_values)]
        for model, preds in model_preds.items()
    }

    return render_template(
        "forecast_vs_real.html",
        dates_all=dates_all, dates_test=dates_test, n_train=n_train,
        train_values=[round(v, 4) for v in train_values],
        test_values=[round(v, 4) for v in test_values],
        model_preds={m: [round(v, 4) for v in p] for m, p in model_preds.items()},
        err_pct_matrix=err_pct_matrix, accuracy_stats=accuracy_stats,
        best_model=best_model, model_names=list(model_preds.keys()), cache_key=cache_key,
    )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Análisis de estacionalidad
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

@bp.route("/seasonality", methods=["POST"])
def seasonality():
    cache_key = request.form.get("cache_key", "")
    cached = cache.get(cache_key) if cache_key else None

    if not cached:
        return "Sesión expirada o inválida. Vuelve a la página principal y consulta de nuevo.", 400

    dates        = cached["dates"]
    train_series = cached["train_series"]
    test_series  = cached["test_series"]

    full_prices = [float(t if t is not None else v) for t, v in zip(train_series, test_series)]
    df = pd.DataFrame({"Date": pd.to_datetime(dates), "Close": full_prices})
    df["Month"]   = df["Date"].dt.month
    df["Quarter"] = df["Date"].dt.quarter
    df["Year"]    = df["Date"].dt.year

    MONTH_NAMES   = ["Ene","Feb","Mar","Abr","May","Jun","Jul","Ago","Sep","Oct","Nov","Dic"]
    QUARTER_NAMES = ["Q1 (Ene–Mar)","Q2 (Abr–Jun)","Q3 (Jul–Sep)","Q4 (Oct–Dic)"]

    mon = df.groupby("Month")["Close"].agg(mean="mean", std="std", min_val="min", max_val="max").reset_index()
    mon["std"]   = mon["std"].fillna(0)
    mon["label"] = mon["Month"].apply(lambda m: MONTH_NAMES[m - 1])

    qtr = df.groupby("Quarter")["Close"].agg(mean="mean", std="std", min_val="min", max_val="max").reset_index()
    qtr["std"]   = qtr["std"].fillna(0)
    qtr["label"] = qtr["Quarter"].apply(lambda q: QUARTER_NAMES[q - 1])

    yr = df.groupby("Year")["Close"].agg(mean="mean", std="std").reset_index()
    yr["std"] = yr["std"].fillna(0)

    insights = {
        "most_volatile_month": mon.loc[int(mon["std"].idxmax()),  "label"],
        "most_volatile_std":   round(float(mon["std"].max()),     4),
        "most_stable_month":   mon.loc[int(mon["std"].idxmin()),  "label"],
        "most_stable_std":     round(float(mon["std"].min()),     4),
        "highest_avg_month":   mon.loc[int(mon["mean"].idxmax()), "label"],
        "highest_avg_val":     round(float(mon["mean"].max()),    4),
        "lowest_avg_month":    mon.loc[int(mon["mean"].idxmin()), "label"],
        "lowest_avg_val":      round(float(mon["mean"].min()),    4),
        "total_years":         int(yr["Year"].nunique()),
        "show_yearly":         int(yr["Year"].nunique()) >= 2,
    }

    return render_template(
        "seasonality.html",
        monthly_labels=mon["label"].tolist(), monthly_mean=mon["mean"].round(4).tolist(),
        monthly_std=mon["std"].round(4).tolist(), monthly_min=mon["min_val"].round(4).tolist(),
        monthly_max=mon["max_val"].round(4).tolist(), quarterly_labels=qtr["label"].tolist(),
        quarterly_mean=qtr["mean"].round(4).tolist(), quarterly_std=qtr["std"].round(4).tolist(),
        yearly_labels=yr["Year"].astype(str).tolist(), yearly_mean=yr["mean"].round(4).tolist(),
        yearly_std=yr["std"].round(4).tolist(), insights=insights, cache_key=cache_key,
    )

══ ARCHIVO: app\static\js\main.js ════════════════════════════════════════════

/**
 * main.js — lógica de la interfaz de Pronóstico USD/MXN
 *
 * Responsabilidades:
 *   1. Inicializar DataTables en la tabla de métricas.
 *   2. Alternar entre el modo de consulta "periodo" y "fechas personalizadas".
 *   3. Pre-rellenar las fechas del modo personalizado con valores razonables.
 *   4. Mostrar un spinner mientras el formulario principal está procesando.
 */

$(document).ready(function () {

    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    // 1. DataTables
    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    if ($('#metrics-table').length) {
        $('#metrics-table').DataTable({
            paging:   false,
            info:     false,
            searching: false,
            ordering: true,
        });
    }

    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    // 2. Toggle entre paneles de modo de consulta
    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    function applyMode(mode) {
        $('#panel_period').hide();
        $('#panel_anchor').hide();
        $('#panel_custom').hide();
        if (mode === 'custom') {
            $('#panel_custom').show();
        } else if (mode === 'anchor') {
            $('#panel_anchor').show();
        } else {
            $('#panel_period').show();
        }
    }

    $('input[name="query_mode"]').on('change', function () {
        applyMode(this.value);
    });

    // Aplica el estado inicial (por si el servidor devuelve un modo distinto al default)
    var initialMode = $('input[name="query_mode"]:checked').val() || 'period';
    applyMode(initialMode);

    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    // 3. Pre-rellenar fechas del modo personalizado
    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    var today = new Date();
    var todayStr = today.toISOString().split('T')[0];

    // Fecha máxima = hoy (para todos los campos de fecha)
    $('#start_date, #end_date, #anchor_date').attr('max', todayStr);

    // Pre-rellena anchor_date con hoy si está vacío
    if (!$('#anchor_date').val()) {
        $('#anchor_date').val(todayStr);
    }

    // Solo pre-rellena si el campo está vacío (no sobrescribe un valor devuelto
    // por el servidor tras un error de validación)
    if (!$('#end_date').val()) {
        $('#end_date').val(todayStr);
    }
    if (!$('#start_date').val()) {
        var oneYearAgo = new Date(today);
        oneYearAgo.setFullYear(oneYearAgo.getFullYear() - 1);
        $('#start_date').val(oneYearAgo.toISOString().split('T')[0]);
    }

    // Evita que end_date sea anterior a start_date
    $('#start_date').on('change', function () {
        if ($('#end_date').val() && $('#end_date').val() < this.value) {
            $('#end_date').val(this.value);
        }
        $('#end_date').attr('min', this.value);
    });

    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    // 4. Spinner de carga
    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    $('#main-form').on('submit', function () {
        $('#btn-submit').prop('disabled', true).text('Procesando…');
        $('#loading-overlay').removeClass('d-none');
    });

    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    // 5. Sincronizar modelo seleccionado con formulario de residuales
    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    function syncModelChoice() {
        var val = $('input[name="model_choice"]:checked').val() || '';
        $('#residuals-model-input').val(val);
    }
    $('input[name="model_choice"]').on('change', syncModelChoice);
    syncModelChoice();

});

<div class="bt-wrap">

    <!-- ============================ Portada ============================ -->
    <section class="bt-hero">
        <div>
            <p class="bt-kicker">Capítulo 4 · Apéndice C · solo lectura</p>
            <h2 class="bt-title">Banco de Tesis</h2>
            <p class="bt-lead">
                Los 54 experimentos con los que se evaluaron los modelos de pronóstico del USD/MXN: seis longitudes de
                historia por nueve particiones de prueba, todas terminando el 31 de diciembre de 2025 (fecha ancla:
                1 de enero de 2026). Esta página reproduce las cifras publicadas tal cual; no entrena ni modifica nada.
            </p>
        </div>
        <div class="bt-stats">
            <div class="bt-stat">
                <span class="bt-stat-label">Periodo cubierto</span>
                <span class="bt-stat-value">{{ span.inicio }} → {{ span.fin }}</span>
                <span class="bt-stat-note">Cierre diario USD/MXN (Yahoo Finance, USDMXN=X); la historia más larga abarca 10 años</span>
            </div>
            <div class="bt-stat">
                <span class="bt-stat-label">Experimentos</span>
                <span class="bt-stat-value">{{ summary.n_elements }}</span>
                <span class="bt-stat-note">6 longitudes de historia × 9 particiones de prueba</span>
            </div>
            <div class="bt-stat">
                <span class="bt-stat-label">Modelos comparados</span>
                <span class="bt-stat-value">6 + 1</span>
                <span class="bt-stat-note">SARIMA, Holt-Winters, Regresión Lineal, Random Forest, RNN y LSTM, más la referencia ingenua (Naive)</span>
            </div>
            <div class="bt-stat">
                <span class="bt-stat-label">Naive en primer lugar</span>
                <span class="bt-stat-value">{{ summary.naive_first }} de {{ summary.n_elements }}</span>
                <span class="bt-stat-note">Solo o empatado, en el ranking con los siete modelos. Victorias significativas frente a Naive: {{ summary.better|length }} de {{ summary.n_tests }} contrastes; {{ summary.better_holm_bank }} al corregir por comparaciones múltiples en todo el banco</span>
            </div>
        </div>
    </section>

    <!-- ============================ Cómo leer ============================ -->
    <section class="bt-card">
        <h3>Cómo está organizado el banco</h3>
        <div class="bt-two">
            <div>
                <p>
                    Cada experimento se identifica con una clave <strong>P,T</strong>. <strong>P</strong> dice cuánta historia
                    se usó (de 3 meses a 10 años hacia atrás desde el 1 de enero de 2026) y <strong>T</strong> qué fracción
                    final de esa historia se apartó para medir el error. Los modelos se estiman solo con la parte de
                    entrenamiento y después predicen, día por día, cada valor del tramo de prueba.
                </p>
                <table class="bt-mini">
                    <thead><tr><th>Clave</th><th>Historia</th><th>Desde</th><th>Hasta</th><th style="text-align:right;">Días hábiles</th></tr></thead>
                    <tbody>
                    {% for p in periods %}
                        <tr><td class="bt-key">{{ p.P }}</td><td>{{ p.label }}</td><td>{{ p.fecha_inicio }}</td><td>{{ p.fecha_fin }}</td><td class="bt-num">{{ p.n }}</td></tr>
                    {% endfor %}
                    </tbody>
                </table>
                <table class="bt-mini">
                    <thead><tr><th>Clave</th>{% for t in tests %}<th style="text-align:center;">{{ t.T }}</th>{% endfor %}</tr></thead>
                    <tbody><tr><td class="bt-key">Prueba</td>{% for t in tests %}<td style="text-align:center; white-space:nowrap;">{{ t.pct }}&nbsp;%</td>{% endfor %}</tr></tbody>
                </table>
            </div>
            <div>
                <dl class="bt-defs">
                    <dt>MAE, RMSE y MAPE</dt>
                    <dd>Errores medios de predicción en pesos (MAE y RMSE) y en porcentaje (MAPE). Menor es mejor; el MAE es la métrica principal de lectura.</dd>
                    <dt>R²</dt>
                    <dd>Fracción de la variación de la serie que el modelo reproduce. Cuando un modelo sigue de cerca el nivel del día anterior, el R² es alto para casi cualquiera; por eso se lee junto con el MAE y no por separado.</dd>
                    <dt>Rank</dt>
                    <dd>Posición promedio en las cuatro métricas. "Rank 6" se calcula entre los seis modelos propuestos (criterio del Capítulo 4); el número de la primera columna incluye además a Naive.</dd>
                    <dt>Naive (modelo ingenuo)</dt>
                    <dd>Pronostica para mañana el valor de hoy. Es la vara de medir: un modelo solo aporta si le gana de forma consistente.</dd>
                    <dt>Prueba DM y veredicto</dt>
                    <dd>La prueba de Diebold-Mariano decide si la diferencia de error frente a Naive es estadísticamente significativa (p &lt; 0.05): <em>mejor</em>, <em>sin diferencia</em> o <em>peor</em>. El <em>p ajustado</em> aplica la corrección de Holm por las seis comparaciones que se hacen en cada experimento; solo una diferencia con p ajustado &lt; 0.05 es robusta a comparaciones múltiples.</dd>
                </dl>
            </div>
        </div>
    </section>

    <!-- ============================ Mapa ============================ -->
    <section class="bt-card">
        <h3>Mapa de resultados</h3>
        <p class="bt-help">
            Una celda por experimento. Arriba, el mejor de los seis modelos propuestos en ese experimento; abajo, cuánto
            menor (−) o mayor (+) fue su error que el del modelo ingenuo. El color resume la comparación con Naive.
            Pasa el cursor para ver los valores y haz clic para abrir el detalle.
        </p>
        <div class="bt-legend">
            <span><i class="bt-swatch bt-sig"></i> ★ supera a Naive con significancia estadística</span>
            <span><i class="bt-swatch bt-better"></i> ▲ menor error que Naive, sin significancia</span>
            <span><i class="bt-swatch bt-equal"></i> = mismo error que Naive</span>
            <span><i class="bt-swatch bt-worse"></i> ▼ mayor error que Naive</span>
        </div>
        <div class="bt-scroll">
        <table class="bt-heat">
            <thead>
                <tr>
                    <th class="bt-corner">Historia ↓ &nbsp;·&nbsp; Prueba →</th>
                    {% for t in tests %}<th>{{ t.T }}<small>{{ t.pct }} %</small></th>{% endfor %}
                </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
            {% for row in heat %}
                <tr>
                    <th class="bt-rowhead">{{ row.P }}<small>{{ row.label }}</small></th>
                    {% for c in row.cells %}
                    <td class="bt-cell bt-{{ c.cls }}" title="{{ c.title }}">
                        <a href="#{{ c.anchor }}" data-p="{{ row.P }}">
                            <span class="bt-cell-model">{{ c.winners|join(' / ') }}</span>
                            <span class="bt-cell-rel">{% if c.cls == 'sig' %}★{% elif c.cls == 'better' %}▲{% elif c.cls == 'equal' %}={% elif c.cls == 'worse' %}▼{% endif %}
                                {% if c.rel is not none %}{% if c.cls == 'equal' %}Naive{% else %}{{ '%+.1f'|format(c.rel) }} %{% endif %}{% else %}—{% endif %}</span>
                        </a>
                    </td>
                    {% endfor %}
                </tr>
            {% endfor %}
            </tbody>
        </table>
        </div>
        <p class="bt-foot">
            Lectura rápida: la única celda con estrella es P1,T9 (Holt-Winters, p = 0.026). Esa ventaja sobrevive a la
            corrección de Holm dentro de su experimento (las otras cinco comparaciones son extremas), pero no al considerar las
            {{ summary.n_tests }} pruebas del banco completo: una sola victoria entre {{ summary.n_tests }} contrastes es compatible con el azar. En el
            resto, el mejor modelo propuesto empata con el paseo aleatorio (SARIMA converge a él cuando auto_arima elige el
            orden (0,1,0)) o la diferencia no alcanza significancia estadística.
        </p>
    </section>

    <!-- ============================ Resúmenes ============================ -->
    <section class="bt-card">
        <h3>Dos resúmenes por modelo</h3>
        <div class="bt-two">
            <div>
                <h4>Primeros lugares por historia</h4>
                <p class="bt-help">
                    Cuántas de las nueve particiones de cada historia ganó cada modelo (ranking entre los seis propuestos,
                    como en las tablas de conteo del Capítulo 4). Los empates cuentan para ambos modelos.
                </p>
                <table class="bt-mini bt-counts">
                    <thead><tr><th>Modelo</th>{% for r in rank1 %}<th title="{{ r.label }}">{{ r.P }}</th>{% endfor %}<th>Total</th></tr></thead>
                    <tbody>
                    {% for m in models6 %}
                        <tr><td>{{ m }}</td>{% for r in rank1 %}<td class="bt-num{% if r.counts[m] == 0 %} bt-zero{% endif %}">{{ r.counts[m] }}</td>{% endfor %}<td class="bt-num bt-total">{{ rank1_total[m] }}</td></tr>
                    {% endfor %}
                        <tr class="bt-naive-row"><td>Naive primero o empatado (con los 7)</td>{% for r in rank1 %}<td class="bt-num">{{ r.naive_first }}</td>{% endfor %}<td class="bt-num bt-total">{{ summary.naive_first }}</td></tr>
                    </tbody>
                </table>
            </div>
            <div>
                <h4>Veredicto frente al modelo ingenuo</h4>
                <p class="bt-help">
                    Resultado de la prueba de Diebold-Mariano en los 54 experimentos: en cuántos fue cada modelo mejor,
                    igual o peor que Naive con significancia al 5 %.
                </p>
                <table class="bt-mini bt-counts">
                    <thead><tr><th>Modelo</th><th>Mejor</th><th title="Mejor que Naive con p ajustado por Holm &lt; 0.05">Mejor (p ajust.)</th><th>Sin diferencia</th><th>Peor</th><th>No calculable</th></tr></thead>
                    <tbody>
                    {% for m in models6 %}
                        <tr>
                            <td>{{ m }}</td>
                            <td class="bt-num {% if dm_counts[m]['mejor que Naive'] %}bt-good{% else %}bt-zero{% endif %}">{{ dm_counts[m]['mejor que Naive'] }}</td>
                            <td class="bt-num {% if dm_counts[m]['mejor (Holm)'] %}bt-good{% else %}bt-zero{% endif %}">{{ dm_counts[m]['mejor (Holm)'] }}</td>
                            <td class="bt-num">{{ dm_counts[m]['sin diferencia'] }}</td>
                            <td class="bt-num {% if dm_counts[m]['peor que Naive'] %}bt-bad{% else %}bt-zero{% endif %}">{{ dm_counts[m]['peor que Naive'] }}</td>
                            <td class="bt-num bt-zero">{{ dm_counts[m]['n/a'] }}</td>
                        </tr>
                    {% endfor %}
                    </tbody>
                </table>
                <p class="bt-foot">"No calculable": las predicciones son idénticas a las de Naive (SARIMA con orden (0,1,0)), por lo que no hay diferencia que probar. "Mejor (p ajust.)": victorias que sobreviven a la corrección de Holm dentro de su experimento ({{ summary.better_holm }} en total); al corregir por las {{ summary.n_tests }} pruebas del banco completo sobreviven {{ summary.better_holm_bank }}.</p>
            </div>
        </div>
    </section>

                            <td class="bt-num">{% if r.is_naive %}referencia{% elif r.rel is not none %}{% if r.rel|abs <= 0.05 %}igual{% else %}{{ '%+.1f'|format(r.rel) }} %{% endif %}{% else %}—{% endif %}</td>
                            <td class="bt-num">{{ '%.4f'|format(r.RMSE) if r.RMSE is not none else '—' }}</td>

                            <td class="bt-num">{{ r.rank6 if r.rank6 else '—' }}</td>
                            <td class="bt-num">{{ '%.4f'|format(r.p) if r.p is not none else '—' }}</td>

<script>
(function () {
    var tabs = Array.prototype.slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('.bt-tab'));
    var panels = Array.prototype.slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('.bt-detail'));
    function show(p) {
        tabs.forEach(function (t) { t.classList.toggle('active', t.dataset.p === p); });
        panels.forEach(function (d) { d.hidden = (d.dataset.p !== p); });
    }
    tabs.forEach(function (t) { t.addEventListener('click', function () { show(t.dataset.p); }); });
    document.querySelectorAll('.bt-heat a').forEach(function (a) {
        a.addEventListener('click', function () { show(a.dataset.p); });
    });
    function fromHash() {
        var k = location.hash.replace('#', '');
        if (k && /^P\d-T\d$/.test(k)) {
            show(k.split('-')[0]);
            var el = document.getElementById(k);
            if (el) { setTimeout(function () { el.scrollIntoView({ block: 'start' }); }, 0); }
        } else {
            show('P1');
        }
    }
    window.addEventListener('hashchange', fromHash);
    fromHash();
})();
</script>

        .accuracy-grid {
            display: grid;
            grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(260px, 1fr));

<script src="https://code.jquery.com/jquery-3.6.0.min.js"></script>
<script src="https://cdn.datatables.net/1.13.8/js/jquery.dataTables.min.js"></script>
<script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/chart.js"></script>
<script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/hammerjs@2.0.8/hammer.min.js"></script>
<script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/chartjs-plugin-zoom@2.0.1/dist/chartjs-plugin-zoom.min.js"></script>
<script>
    const datesAll    = {{ dates_all|tojson }};
    const datesTest   = {{ dates_test|tojson }};
    const nTrain      = {{ n_train }};
    const trainValues = {{ train_values|tojson }};
    const testValues  = {{ test_values|tojson }};
    const modelPreds  = {{ model_preds|tojson }};
    const errMatrix   = {{ err_pct_matrix|tojson }};
    const modelNames  = {{ model_names|tojson }};
    const bestModel   = {{ best_model|tojson }};

    // Build full-length arrays (train + test) aligned with datesAll
    const nTotal = datesAll.length;
    const nTest  = testValues.length;

    // Training context: real values for train period, null for test period
    const trainFull = trainValues.concat(new Array(nTest).fill(null));

    // Real test values padded with nulls at the start
    const testFull  = new Array(nTrain).fill(null).concat(testValues);

    // Paleta de colores para los modelos
    const palette = [
        { border: '#6B7280', bg: 'rgba(107,114,128,0.08)', dash: [2,3] },
        { border: '#C8A800', bg: 'rgba(200,168,0,0.08)',   dash: [6,3] },
        { border: '#5B2D8E', bg: 'rgba(91,45,142,0.08)',   dash: [4,4] },
        { border: '#E05A1E', bg: 'rgba(224,90,30,0.08)',   dash: [6,3] },
        { border: '#1E7B8C', bg: 'rgba(30,123,140,0.08)',  dash: [4,4] },
        { border: '#9B2335', bg: 'rgba(155,35,53,0.08)',   dash: [6,3] },
        { border: '#2E86AB', bg: 'rgba(46,134,171,0.08)',  dash: [4,4] },
    ];

    // ── Gráfica comparativa (rango completo seleccionado) ──
    const datasets = [
        {
            label: 'Entrenamiento',
            data: trainFull,
            borderColor: 'rgba(0,48,135,0.4)',
            backgroundColor: 'rgba(0,48,135,0.04)',
            borderWidth: 1.5,
            pointRadius: 0,
            fill: false,
            spanGaps: false,
            tension: 0.2,
            order: 10,
        },
        {
            label: 'Real (prueba)',
            data: testFull,
            borderColor: '#1A7340',
            backgroundColor: 'rgba(26,115,64,0.08)',
            borderWidth: 2.5,
            pointRadius: 2,
            pointBackgroundColor: '#1A7340',
            fill: false,
            spanGaps: false,
            tension: 0.2,
            order: 1,
        }
    ];

    modelNames.forEach((name, i) => {
        const c = palette[i % palette.length];
        // Pad predictions with nulls for the training period
        const predFull = new Array(nTrain).fill(null).concat(modelPreds[name]);
        datasets.push({
            label: name,
            data: predFull,
            borderColor: c.border,
            backgroundColor: c.bg,
            borderWidth: 1.8,
            borderDash: c.dash,
            pointRadius: 0,
            fill: false,
            spanGaps: false,
            tension: 0.2,
            order: i + 2,
        });
    });

    new Chart(document.getElementById('chart-compare'), {
        type: 'line',
        data: { labels: datesAll, datasets },
        options: {
            responsive: true,
            interaction: { mode: 'index', intersect: false },
            plugins: {
                legend: {
                    position: 'top',
                    labels: { font: { size: 12, weight: '600' }, color: '#0D1B3E', usePointStyle: true, padding: 16 }
                },
                title: {
                    display: true,
                    text: 'USD/MXN — Rango completo seleccionado: Entrenamiento · Real (prueba) · Pronósticos',
                    color: '#003087',
                    font: { size: 13, weight: '700' },
                    padding: { bottom: 14 },
                },
                tooltip: {
                    backgroundColor: 'rgba(0,31,90,0.92)', titleColor: '#F0CC30',
                    bodyColor: '#FFFFFF', borderColor: '#C8A800', borderWidth: 1, padding: 10,
                    titleFont: { weight: '700' },
                    filter: item => item.raw !== null,
                },
                zoom: {
                    zoom: { wheel: { enabled: true, speed: 0.1 }, pinch: { enabled: true }, mode: 'x' },
                    pan:  { enabled: true, mode: 'x' },
                    limits: { x: { minRange: 5 } },
                }
            },
            scales: {
                x: {
                    ticks: { maxTicksLimit: 18, maxRotation: 45, color: '#4A5568', font: { size: 10 } },
                    grid: { color: 'rgba(0,48,135,0.06)' }, border: { color: '#D6DCE8' },
                },
                y: {
                    ticks: { color: '#4A5568', font: { size: 11 }, callback: v => 'MXN ' + v.toFixed(2) },
                    grid: { color: 'rgba(0,48,135,0.06)' }, border: { color: '#D6DCE8' },
                    title: { display: true, text: 'Tipo de cambio (MXN/USD)', color: '#4A5568', font: { size: 10, weight: '600' } },
                }
            }
        }
    });

    // ── Tabla de detalle ──
    function errClass(e) {
        if (e <= 1)  return 'err-low';
        if (e <= 2)  return 'err-medium';
        return 'err-high';
    }

    const tbody = document.getElementById('detail-tbody');
    datesTest.forEach((date, i) => {
        const tr = document.createElement('tr');
        // Fecha
        let td = document.createElement('td');
        td.textContent = date;
        tr.appendChild(td);
        // Real
        td = document.createElement('td');
        td.textContent = testValues[i].toFixed(4);
        td.style.fontWeight = '600';
        tr.appendChild(td);
        // Cada modelo
        modelNames.forEach(model => {
            td = document.createElement('td');
            const pred = modelPreds[model][i];
            const err  = errMatrix[model][i];
            td.textContent = pred.toFixed(4);
            td.className = errClass(err);
            td.title = `Error: ${err.toFixed(3)} %`;
            tr.appendChild(td);
        });
        tbody.appendChild(tr);
    });

    $('#detail-table').DataTable({
        paging:    true,
        pageLength: 20,
        lengthMenu: [10, 20, 50, 100],
        info:      true,
        searching: false,
        ordering:  false,
        language: {
            paginate: { previous: 'Anterior', next: 'Siguiente' },
            info:     'Mostrando _START_–_END_ de _TOTAL_ observaciones',
            lengthMenu: 'Mostrar _MENU_ filas',
        }
    });
</script>
</body>
</html>
